**业务逻辑：**

1. **参数校验。** `patient_id > 0`；`category_codes` 各值必须在 `ScopeKind` 枚举内；`product_codes` / `charge_codes` 各 ≤50 项；`service_actions` ≤6 项；`settlement_base >= 0`。违规返回 `"40001"`。
2. **按患者查档案。** 查 `m10_member_profile`（走 `uk_m10_member_profile_patient`）。
   - **不存在档案** → 返回 `is_member = false`、`benefits = []`、`packages = []`、`level = null`，`code = 0`。**这是正常业务状态，不是错误**——视光中心大量顾客不是会员，M16-06 需要能正常试算（按普通客户）。
3. **会员状态判定。** 查 `m10_member_level_snap`（`is_current = TRUE`）取等级与权益参数。
   - 若 `member_status != 'ACTIVE'`（M03 冻结/过期/注销）或 `profile_status != 'ACTIVE'`：置 `benefits_available = false`，`level` 照常返回（供小票展示等级），`benefits` 返回空数组，`packages` **仍返回**（已付钱的套餐可核销，但本接口是给"优惠试算"用的，故套餐在这里只返回元数据，不参与折扣）。
   - 若快照 `is_stale = TRUE` 且陈旧时长 > `LEVEL_SNAP_MAX_STALE_MIN` → 返回 `"1008007"`（见降级策略 §8.7）。
4. **合并视光专属权益。** 查 `m10_member_benefit`（`is_enabled = TRUE`、有效期覆盖今天、`min_level_seq <= 当前 level_seq`），逐条做**硬性过滤**：
   - `require_child_myopia = TRUE` 且 `profile.is_child_myopia = FALSE` → 排除；
   - `require_orthok = TRUE` 且 `profile.is_orthok_wearer = FALSE` → 排除；
   - **请求带 `category_codes` 时**，`scope_kind` 不在其中且不为 `ALL`/`OPTICAL` → 排除；
   - 请求带 `service_actions` 时，`benefit_type = 'FREE_SERVICE'` 且 `scope_value` 与请求动作无交集 → 排除。
5. **计算 `times_remaining`。** `times_limit IS NULL` → `null`（不限次）；否则 `times_remaining = times_limit − times_used_by_member`，若为 0 则 `available = false`、`unavailable_reason = '本周期次数已用完'`。
6. **组装套餐参数。** 查 `m10_member_package_sale`（`sale_status = 'ACTIVE'`、`paid_status = 'PAID'`、未过期），逐卡计算每项的 `remaining_times` / `remaining_qty` / `period_remaining` / `cooldown_until`（口径见 §3.4.1、§3.4.4）。
7. **计算 `package_extendable_days`。** `max_extend_days − extended_days`，用于告知 M16-06「该会员还有多少延长额度可用」。
8. **绝不计算金额。** 响应中不出现任何 `payable`、`discount_amount`、`final_price` 字段。`settlement_base` 仅用于「满额门槛型权益」的可用性判定（如某权益配了 `min_settlement_amount`，本设计当前未启用该字段，保留扩展位）。
9. **写缓存。** 结果写入 Redis `m10:member:benefit:{patient_id}:{digest}` TTL `M10.MEMBER.BENEFIT_CACHE_TTL`（默认 300 秒），`digest` 为当前快照的 `benefit_digest`；快照变更时主动 `DEL`。
10. **埋点。** 记录 `elapsed_ms` 到监控指标 `m10_member_p22_latency`。

**性能：** P95 ≤ 100ms（缓存命中 ≤5ms）；QPS 预估 峰值 40。

**调用方与频次：** M16-06 每次优惠试算调用一次（视光中心日均 184 单 × 1.6 次试算 ≈ 295 次/日，峰值 40 QPS）。

**`benefits_available = false` 时的语义边界（极其重要）：**

| 场景 | 是否返回权益参数 | M16-06 应如何处理 | 返回字段 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 非会员（无档案） | 否 | 按普通客户计（无等级折扣） | `is_member = false` |
| 会员状态 `FROZEN` | 否 | 不应用会员折扣，但套餐仍可核销 | `benefits_available = false` |
| 会员状态 `EXPIRED` | 否 | 同冻结 | 同上 |
| 档案 `SUSPENDED` | 否 | 同冻结 | 同上 |
| 档案 `CLOSED` | 否 | 同冻结 | 同上 + `profile_status = 'CLOSED'` |
| 快照陈旧 > 60 分钟 | 否 | 按普通客户计（**降级放行，不阻塞收银**） | `"1008007"` 或空权益 |
| M03-03 完全不可达 | 是（用陈旧快照） | 正常试算 + `degraded = true` | `degraded_reason = 'M03_UNAVAILABLE'` |

#### API-34 P-23 消费完成回写（内部契约）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/members/on-consumed
X-Internal-Token: ***
Idempotency-Key: 3f2a1b9c-4d5e-6f70-8192-a3b4c5d6e7f8
```

```json
{
  "source_type": "ORDER_DELIVER",
  "source_biz_no": "SO202610310021",
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "member_no": "M202610200001",
  "sales_order_no": "SO202610310021",
  "optical_order_no": "PO202610310021",
  "used_at": "2026-11-02T10:15:00+08:00",
  "operator_id": 5001,
  "items": [
    {
      "package_sale_no": "MS202610310007",
      "item_code": "PKG-MYOPIA-ANNUAL-I02",
      "use_qty": 1.00,
      "patient_id": 10021
    },
    {
      "service_action": "CLEAN",
      "use_qty": 1.00,
      "patient_id": 10021
    }
  ],
  "auto_create_profile": true,
  "remark": "取镜时核销离焦镜片并做镜片清洗"
}
```

| 字段 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `source_type` | string | 是 | `ORDER_DELIVER` / `FOLLOWUP` / `SERVICE` / `GOODS_PICKUP` / `MANUAL`；`RETURN` 请走 API-35 |
| `source_biz_no` | string | 是 | 来源业务单号（销售单号 / 复查计划号 / 服务单号） |
| `patient_id` | int | 是 | 患者 ID（受益患者，全家套餐可不同于会员持有人） |
| `member_no` | string | 否 | 会员号；为空时按 `patient_id` 反查（见逻辑 2） |
| `sales_order_no` | string | 否 | M16-04 销售单号 |
| `optical_order_no` | string | 否 | M10-06 订单号 |
| `used_at` | datetime | 否 | 核销业务时间，默认当前时间 |
| `operator_id` | int | 否 | 操作人 |
| `items[].package_sale_no` | string | 条件 | 指定卡号；为空则按 `item_code`/`service_action` 自动匹配该患者全部 ACTIVE 卡 |
| `items[].item_code` | string | 条件 | 内容项编码，与 `service_action` 二选一 |
| `items[].service_action` | string | 条件 | 服务动作，与 `item_code` 二选一 |
| `items[].use_qty` | decimal | 是 | 核销数量（>0） |
| `items[].patient_id` | int | 否 | 单项受益患者，默认取顶层 `patient_id` |
| `auto_create_profile` | boolean | 否 | 视光档案不存在时是否自动创建，默认 `true` |
| `remark` | string | 否 | 备注 |

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "member_no": "M202610200001",
    "source_type": "ORDER_DELIVER",
    "source_biz_no": "SO202610310021",
    "results": [
      {
        "use_no": "MU20261102000012",
        "sale_no": "MS202610310007",
        "item_code": "PKG-MYOPIA-ANNUAL-I02",
        "item_type": "GOODS",
        "use_type": "CONSUME",
        "use_qty": 1.00,
        "usage_seq": 1,
        "quota_before": 1.00,
        "quota_after": 0.00,
        "sale_status": "ACTIVE",
        "is_idempotent": false,
        "used_at": "2026-11-02T10:15:00+08:00"
      },
      {
        "use_no": "MU20261102000013",
        "sale_no": "MS202610310007",
        "item_code": "PKG-MYOPIA-ANNUAL-I05",
        "item_type": "SERVICE",
        "use_type": "CONSUME",
        "use_qty": 1.00,
        "usage_seq": 7,
        "quota_before": null,
        "quota_after": null,
        "sale_status": "ACTIVE",
        "is_idempotent": false,
        "used_at": "2026-11-02T10:15:00+08:00"
      }
    ],
    "profile_created": false,
    "profile_updated": true,
    "skipped": [],
    "used_quota_total": 3.00,
    "remaining_ratio": 57.14,
    "published_events": ["m10.member.package.used"]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑（按执行顺序）：**

1. **参数校验。** `items` 非空且 ≤20 项；每项 `use_qty > 0`；`item_code` 与 `service_action` 二选一；`source_type` 不允许 `RETURN`（返回 `"40001"` 并提示改用 API-35）。
2. **定位会员与档案。**
   - 有 `member_no` → 按号查 `m10_member_profile`；
   - 无 `member_no` → 调 U-01 `GET /internal/v1/member/by-patient/{patient_id}` 拿会员号，再查档案；
   - 档案不存在且 `auto_create_profile = true` → **自动建档**（从 M03 会员 + M16-04 销售单取信息），`profile_created = true`；
   - 档案不存在且 `auto_create_profile = false` → 返回 `"1008001"`，但**不阻塞核销**（继续执行，档案缺失只影响 P-21 展示）。
3. **解析每个核销项。**
   - 指定 `package_sale_no` → 直接定位该卡；
   - 未指定 → 在该会员全部 `sale_status = 'ACTIVE'` 卡中，按 `item_code` 精确匹配，或按 `service_action` 匹配 `item_type = 'SERVICE'` 且 `service_action` 相同的项；
   - 匹配到多项（多张同类卡）→ 按 `effective_expire_date` 升序（先用快到期的）取第一张；
   - **匹配不到 → 不进 `results`，进 `skipped`**，`reason = 'NO_MATCHING_PACKAGE_ITEM'`。**不报错**，因为 M16 并不知道患者有没有套餐，它只是「尽力回写」。
4. **逐项核销（每项一个独立事务）。** 对每一项：
   1. `SELECT ... FROM m10_member_package_sale WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE`；
   2. 校验卡状态 `ACTIVE`（`PENDING`/`EXPIRED`/`FROZEN`/`EXHAUSTED` → 进 `skipped`，`reason` 为具体状态）；
   3. **幂等检查**（双钥匙）：
      - `idempotency_key = {source_type}:{source_biz_no}:{sale_no}:{item_code}:{usage_seq}`；
      - 但 P-23 的 `usage_seq` 由 M10 内部计算（**调用方不传**），因此幂等键在匹配完成后才生成；
      - 若请求头带 `Idempotency-Key`，则 `idempotency_key = 'HEADER:' + 头值 + ':' + item_code`，`idempotency_scope = 'HEADER'`；
      - 命中已有流水 → 返回该流水 + `is_idempotent = true`；
   4. 计算 `usage_seq`：`MAX(usage_seq) WHERE sale_id = ? AND item_id = ? AND use_type = 'CONSUME'` + 1（无则为 1）；
   5. 校验额度：`remaining = total − SUM(use_qty) ≥ use_qty`，否则进 `skipped`（`reason = 'QUOTA_EXHAUSTED'`）；
   6. 校验周期与冷却（口径 §3.4.4），不通过进 `skipped`（`reason = 'PERIOD_LIMIT'` / `'COOLDOWN'`）；
   7. `INSERT m10_member_package_use`（`use_type='CONSUME'`，含 `quota_before` / `quota_after`）；
   8. `UPDATE m10_member_package_item.used_times / used_qty`（冗余）；
   9. `UPDATE m10_member_package_sale.used_quota / remaining_ratio`（冗余）；
   10. 若全部计额度项剩余 = 0 → `UPDATE sale_status = 'EXHAUSTED' + exhausted_at`。
5. **回写会员档案视光属性。**
   - `last_visit_at = used_at`；
   - 若 `source_type = 'ORDER_DELIVER'` → 按订单号去重，`fit_count += 1`，`last_fit_date = used_at::date`，`last_fit_order_no = optical_order_no`，若 `first_fit_date IS NULL` 则同时置为同日。
6. **发布事件。** 提交成功后发布 `m10.member.package.used`（每项一条）；若档案属性变化则发布 `m10.member.profile.updated`。
7. **返回聚合结果。** `used_quota_total` 与 `remaining_ratio` 取主卡（`results[0]` 的卡）的最新值。

**幂等的三个层次（本接口最容易出错的地方）：**

```
第 1 层  Redis m10:idem:member_use:{idempotency_key}  NX EX 86400
         └── 命中 → 查 DB 返回原流水，is_idempotent = true
第 2 层  DB uk_m10_pkg_use_idem(idempotency_key, created_at)
         └── 命中唯一冲突 → 捕获后查 DB 返回原流水
第 3 层  DB uk_m10_pkg_use_occur(sale_id, item_id, usage_seq) WHERE use_type='CONSUME'
         └── 即使第 1/2 层因调用方参数变化而失效，同一卡同一项同一序号仍只能写一次
```

**为什么第 3 层必须存在：** 因为 M16-04 重试时可能把 `source_biz_no` 从 `SO202610310021` 改成 `SO202610310021-R1`（例如重试队列加了后缀）。第 1/2 层的幂等键会因参数不同而失效，但第 3 层用「卡 ID + 项 ID + 序号」三元组兜底，这三个值都由 M10 内部生成，可靠。

**部分成功语义：**

| 情况 | HTTP | `code` | 行为 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 全部项核销成功 | 200 | 0 | `results` 全量返回 |
| 部分项成功、部分跳过 | 200 | 0 | `results` 为成功的，`skipped` 为跳过的（含原因） |
| 全部项跳过（无匹配/额度不足/状态不符） | 200 | 0 | `results = []`，`skipped` 全量；**不报错** |
| 参数非法 | 400 | `"40001"` | — |
| 幂等键被用于不同内容项 | 200 | `"1008040"` | 返回原流水号 |
| 数据库不可用 | 500 | `"50000"` | 全部回滚 |

> **为什么"全部跳过"不报错？** 因为 P-23 是「尽力回写」。M16-04 在取镜时会调它，但顾客可能根本没买套餐（视光中心 70% 的订单没有套餐）。若此时返回错误，M16-04 会认为取镜失败并回滚交易——这是灾难性的。设计上明确：**核销是"顺带做的事"，失败不影响主流程**。

**性能：** P95 ≤ 150ms（单卡单事务 + 2 次 UPDATE）；QPS 峰值 15。

**M16-04 是否允许调用失败？** 允许。M16-04 的取镜流程不依赖 P-23 的返回值；本模块提供每日 02:00 的 `job_member_usage_reconcile` 补偿任务，扫「已交付但无核销流水」的订单并补核销。

---
#### API-01 会员工作台聚合

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/members/workbench?stat_date=2026-10-31&store_id=0
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "stat_date": "2026-10-31",
    "store_id": 0,
    "cards": {
      "total_member_cnt": 6182,
      "new_member_cnt": 16,
      "fit_order_cnt": 184,
      "active_package_cnt": 12,
      "pending_quota": 1284,
      "my_care_cnt": 38
    },
    "todos": [
      {"care_no": "MC2026103100123", "care_type": "PACKAGE_EXPIRE", "priority": 1,
       "member_name": "张**", "level_code": "GOLD", "due_date": "2026-10-31",
       "summary": "护理液套餐剩 30 天"},
      {"care_no": "MC2026103100124", "care_type": "CHILD_FOLLOWUP", "priority": 2,
       "member_name": "李**", "level_code": "SILVER", "due_date": "2026-10-31",
       "summary": "已 96 天未复查"}
    ],
    "alerts": [
      {"level": "WARN", "type": "LEVEL_SNAP_STALE", "count": 3,
       "message": "等级快照陈旧 3 人，M03-03 同步延迟超 60 分钟", "jump": "/m10/member/profiles?stale=1"},
      {"level": "WARN", "type": "PRODUCT_DISABLED", "count": 1,
       "message": "OPLN000456 已停用，影响 PKG-MYOPIA-ANNUAL", "jump": "/m10/member/packages"},
      {"level": "ERROR", "type": "PACKAGE_UNPAID", "count": 2,
       "message": "未付款套餐 2 张已超 24 小时", "jump": "/m10/member/package-sales?paid_status=UNPAID"},
      {"level": "ERROR", "type": "QUOTA_MISMATCH", "count": 1,
       "message": "核销额度对账差异 1 笔", "jump": "/m10/member/package-uses?mismatch=1"}
    ],
    "trend_7d": [
      {"date": "2026-10-25", "new_member": 14, "fit": 21, "use": 46, "convert": 3},
      {"date": "2026-10-26", "new_member": 18, "fit": 27, "use": 52, "convert": 4}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 读 `m10_member_stat`（`stat_period='DAY'`, `stat_date`, `store_id`, `level_code='ALL'`）取卡片；2) 取当前登录人可见的 `PENDING`+`ASSIGNED` 任务，按 `priority, due_date` 取前 20；3) 4 类告警分别用轻量 COUNT 查询（走部分索引）；4) 近 7 日趋势从 `m10_member_stat` 取 7 行；5) 结果缓存 60 秒，Key `m10:member:workbench:{store_id}:{stat_date}:{user_id}`。**错误码：** `"40001"`（日期晚于今天）、`"109001"`。

#### API-02 会员档案分页列表

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/members/profiles?keyword=王&level_code=GOLD&profile_status=ACTIVE&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "member_no": "M202610200001",
        "patient_id": 10021,
        "member_name": "王**",
        "member_phone_mask": "138****5678",
        "level_code": "GOLD",
        "level_seq": 3,
        "member_status": "ACTIVE",
        "profile_status": "ACTIVE",
        "fit_count": 4,
        "last_fit_date": "2026-09-18",
        "is_child_myopia": false,
        "is_orthok_wearer": false,
        "care_opt_in": true,
        "level_is_stale": false
      }
    ],
    "total": 6182,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 310
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按条件拼 SQL：`keyword` 命中会员号前缀 → `idx_m10_member_profile_no`，命中姓名 → `idx_m10_member_profile_name_trgm`；2) 等级/状态/门店走 `idx_m10_member_profile_level` 与 `idx_m10_member_profile_store`；3) 儿童防控走部分索引 `idx_m10_member_profile_child`；4) `level_is_stale` 由快照 `synced_at` 与阈值比较得出（LEFT JOIN `m10_member_level_snap` `is_current=TRUE`）；5) 手机号强制掩码（DTO 层）。**错误码：** `"40001"`、`"109001"`。

#### API-04 开通视光会员档案

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/members/profiles
Idempotency-Key: 8c1f0a2e-3b4d-5e6f-7a8b-9c0d1e2f3a4b
```

```json
{
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "store_id": 101,
  "is_child_myopia": false,
  "myopia_control_no": null,
  "primary_optometrist_id": 2001,
  "care_opt_in": true,
  "remark": "老客户推荐开通"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 40001,
    "member_no": "M202610200001",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "profile_status": "ACTIVE",
    "level_code": "GOLD",
    "is_idempotent": false,
    "created_at": "2026-10-31T10:20:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键检查（Redis NX）；2) 调 U-01 按 `patient_id` 查 M03 会员 → 不存在返回 `"1008002"`；3) 会员 `status = 'CANCELLED'` → `"1008004"`；4) 查本模块是否已有档案 → 有则 `"1008003"` 并返回已有档案号；5) 校验儿童防控/OK 镜必填项（`"1008005"`/`"1008006"`）；6) 调 U-03 取患者姓名/生日/性别快照；7) 调 U-01 取等级并 `INSERT m10_member_level_snap`（`snap_version = 1`，`sync_source = 'PULL'`）；8) `INSERT m10_member_profile`；9) 写审计 `sys_audit_log`；10) 发布 `m10.member.profile.created`。**错误码：** `"1008002"`、`"1008003"`、`"1008004"`、`"1008005"`、`"1008006"`、`"1008039"`、`"40900"`。

#### API-05 修改视光专属属性

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/members/profiles/M202610200001
```

```json
{
  "store_id": 101,
  "primary_optometrist_id": 2001,
  "preferred_frame_size": "54-18-145",
  "preference_tags": ["离焦镜片", "高折射率"],
  "care_opt_in": true,
  "remark": "",
  "version": 3
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"member_no": "M202610200001", "version": 4, "updated_fields": ["preferred_frame_size"]},
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 乐观锁校验 `version`，不一致返回 `"40900"`；2) **只允许修改视光专属属性**——请求体中若出现 `member_no` / `level_code` / `member_status` / `fit_count` / `total_fit_amount_snap` 等字段，一律忽略并记 `WARN` 日志（Pydantic 模型层面已不可传，此处为双保险）；3) 校验镜架尺寸格式；4) 更新后发布 `m10.member.profile.updated`。**错误码：** `"1008001"`、`"1008005"`、`"1008006"`、`"40900"`。

#### API-06 手动刷新等级与权益快照

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/members/profiles/M202610200001/refresh-level
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "member_no": "M202610200001",
    "old_snap_version": 4,
    "new_snap_version": 4,
    "changed": false,
    "level_code": "GOLD",
    "benefit_digest": "9b2c8f1e4a7d0b3c6e5f8a1d2c9b0e7f4a3d6c1b8e5f2a9d0c7b4e1f8a5d2c9b",
    "sync_source": "MANUAL",
    "synced_at": "2026-10-31T10:25:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 调 U-01 取最新等级；2) 调 U-02 取最新通用权益参数；3) 合并本模块视光专属权益，计算 `benefit_digest`；4) **与当前快照 `benefit_digest` 相同时不产生新版本**，只更新 `synced_at`、`is_stale = FALSE`（避免快照表被无意义版本刷爆）；5) 不同时：旧版本 `is_current = FALSE` + `effective_to = now()`，插入新版本 `snap_version + 1`；6) 清缓存 `m10:member:benefit:*`。**错误码：** `"1008001"`、`"1008039"`（M03 不可用）。

#### API-07 等级与权益快照版本历史

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/members/profiles/M202610200001/level-history?page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {"snap_version": 4, "level_code": "GOLD", "level_seq": 3, "level_change_type": "UP",
       "effective_from": "2026-10-31T09:30:12+08:00", "effective_to": null, "is_current": true,
       "optical_discount_ratio": 0.9000, "sync_source": "EVENT"},
      {"snap_version": 3, "level_code": "SILVER", "level_seq": 2, "level_change_type": "BENEFIT",
       "effective_from": "2026-06-01T00:00:00+08:00", "effective_to": "2026-10-31T09:30:12+08:00",
       "is_current": false, "optical_discount_ratio": 0.9500, "sync_source": "PULL"}
    ],

    "pages": 1
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `member_no` + `snap_version DESC` 查快照全量版本（含历史，**不删除**）；2) `is_current` 标记当前版本；3) 返回等级变更类型中文映射由前端处理。**错误码：** `"1008001"`。

#### API-08 挂起视光档案

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/members/profiles/M202610200001/suspend
```

```json
{
  "reason": "会员存在未处理投诉，暂停视光权益发放"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"member_no": "M202610200001", "profile_status": "SUSPENDED", "suspended_at": "2026-10-31T10:30:00+08:00"},
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限校验 `m10:member:manage`；2) 档案必须 `ACTIVE`（`"1008004"`）；3) `reason` 去空格后 ≥5 字（`"1008031"`）；4) 置 `profile_status = 'SUSPENDED'`，把原因写入 `remark` 前缀 `[SUSPEND]`；5) **不影响的**：已购套餐核销、M03 会员状态；6) 清缓存并发布 `m10.member.profile.updated`；7) 写审计。**错误码：** `"1008001"`、`"1008004"`、`"1008031"`、`"109001"`。

#### API-09 恢复视光档案

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/members/profiles/M202610200001/resume
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"member_no": "M202610200001", "profile_status": "ACTIVE", "resumed_at": "2026-10-31T11:00:00+08:00"},
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 档案必须 `SUSPENDED`；2) `CLOSED` 不可恢复（`"1008032"`）；3) 恢复后清缓存、发布事件、写审计。**错误码：** `"1008001"`、`"1008032"`。

#### API-10 会员卡 / 套餐卡 / 核销凭证打印数据

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/members/profiles/print
```

```json
{
  "template": "PACKAGE_CARD",
  "member_nos": ["M202610200001"],
  "sale_nos": ["MS202610310007", "MS202610120003"],
  "use_nos": [],
  "copies": 1
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "template": "PACKAGE_CARD",
    "total": 2,
    "cards": [
      {
        "sale_no": "MS202610310007",
        "member_no": "M202610200001",
        "member_name": "王**",
        "level_name": "金卡会员",
        "package_name": "儿童近视防控年卡",
        "package_version": 2,
        "start_date": "2026-10-31",
        "effective_expire_date": "2027-10-30",
        "remaining_ratio": 71.43,
        "watermark": null,
        "qr_content": "MS202610310007",
        "service_hotline": "0000-0000000",
        "items": [
          {"item_name": "综合验光（含散瞳）", "display_quota": "2 次"},
          {"item_name": "离焦镜片", "display_quota": "1 副"},
          {"item_name": "视力复查", "display_quota": "4 次"},
          {"item_name": "镜架调整", "display_quota": "不限次"},
          {"item_name": "镜片清洗", "display_quota": "不限次"}
        ]
      }
    ],
    "failed": []
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `template` 分派：`PACKAGE_CARD` 需 `sale_nos`；`MEMBER_BENEFIT_CARD` 需 `member_nos`；`REDEEM_RECEIPT` 需 `use_nos`；`CARE_NOTE` 需 `member_nos` + `care_type`；2) 单次最多 200 张（`"1008036"`）；3) 卡状态 `EXPIRED` → `watermark = '已过期'`；`remaining_ratio = 0` → `watermark = '额度已用完'`；4) 遍历 `item_snapshot` 生成 `display_quota` 文案（次数型 `N 次`、商品型 `N 单位`、服务型 `不限次` 或 `N 次`）；5) 写打印日志 `interface_print_log` + 审计。**错误码：** `"1008008"`、`"1008035"`、`"1008036"`、`"109001"`。


**业务逻辑：** 1) 按编码查，`version_no` 为空取 `is_active = TRUE` 的版本；2) 联查内容项（`is_enabled = TRUE` 按 `sort_no`）；3) 返回完整的退换与叠加策略，**错误码：** `"1008009"`。

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `approval_no` 必填且调 M01-11 校验审批已通过（`"1008016"`）；2) 同一 `package_code` 的旧在售版本置 `is_active = FALSE`（`uk_m10_member_package_active` 保证唯一）；3) 新版本 `is_active = TRUE`；4) 清套餐缓存；5) 写审计。**错误码：** `"1008009"`、`"1008016"`、`"109001"`。

```json
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "sale_no": "MS202610310007",
    "member_no": "M202610200001",
    "package_code": "PKG-MYOPIA-ANNUAL",
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键检查；2) 会员档案必须存在且 `ACTIVE`（`"1008004"`）；3) 套餐必须在售或曾售（`"1008009"`）；4) 准入校验：`min_level_seq`（等级）、`require_age_min/max`（按 `birth_date` 算年龄）、`require_child_myopia`、`require_orthok` → 不满足返回 `"1008021"` 并列出具体条件；5) 调 U-06 校验销售单存在且已付清（`"1008020"`）；6) `uk_m10_pkg_sale_order` 校验同单同套餐未登记（`"1008019"`）；7) 复制当前在售版本的 `item_snapshot`（**冻结内容项**）；8) 计算 `start_date` / `expire_date` / `total_quota`；9) 插入 `m10_member_package_sale`（`sale_status = 'ACTIVE'` 或 `'PENDING'`）；10) 发布 `m10.member.package.sold`；11) 写审计。**错误码：** `"1008004"`、`"1008009"`、`"1008019"`、`"1008020"`、`"1008021"`。

FOR UPDATE` 锁卡（串行化并发核销）；3) 卡状态必须 `ACTIVE`（`"1008024"` / `"1008030"`）；4) 幂等三重检查；5) 计算 `usage_seq`；6) 额度校验（`"1008025"`）；7) 周期校验（`"1008022"`）；8) 冷却校验（`"1008023"`）；9) `dry_run` 时只返回校验结果不落库；10) 落库三步（INSERT 流水 + 更新 item 冗余 + 更新 sale 冗余）+ 状态流转 `EXHAUSTED`；11) 提交流程后发布 `m10.member.package.used`。**错误码：** `"1008008"`、`"1008009"`、`"1008022"`、`"1008023"`、`"1008024"`、`"1008025"`、`"1008030"`、`"1008040"`。

**业务逻辑：** 1) 卡必须 `ACTIVE`；2) `reason` ≥5 字（`"1008026"`）；3) 置 `FROZEN`；4) 冻结后不可核销但**不影响已核销的记录**；5) 写审计。**错误码：** `"1008008"`、`"1008024"`、`"1008026"`。

#### API-23 冲减核销

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/members/package-sales/MS202610310007/reverse
```

```json
{
  "refund_no": "RF202611100002",
  "reverse_scope": "PARTIAL",
  "item_codes": ["PKG-MYOPIA-ANNUAL-I02"],
  "reverse_reason": "PARTIAL_RETURN",
  "free_residual_policy": "KEEP",
  "use_nos": ["MU20261031000008"]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "sale_no": "MS202610310007",
    "sale_status": "ACTIVE",
    "reversed": [
      {"use_no": "MU20261110000031", "use_type": "REVERSE", "use_qty": -1.00,
       "reversed_use_id": 88012, "quota_before": 0.00, "quota_after": 1.00}
    ],
    "remaining_ratio": 71.43,
    "used_quota": 2.00,
    "already_reversed_use_nos": []
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m10:member:manage`；2) 校验 `refund_no` 在 M16-08 存在（调 U-06 家族接口）；3) `reverse_scope = 'PARTIAL'` 必须有 `item_codes`，`'FULL'` 不得有；4) `used_ratio_limit` 判定（`"1008027"`）；5) `allow_return = FALSE` → `"1008028"`；6) 逐条定位原 CONSUME 行（`use_nos` 指定或按 `item_codes` 匹配）；7) **每条原行插入一条 REVERSE 行**（`use_qty = -原值`，`reversed_use_id = 原行 id`，`usage_seq` 复用）；8) 若 `reversed_use_id` 已存在 REVERSE（`uk_m10_pkg_use_reverse`）→ 该行进 `already_reversed_use_nos`，**不报错**；9) 依 `free_residual_policy` 决定额度是否加回；10) 更新卡状态（`RETURNED`/恢复 `ACTIVE`）；11) 写审计。**错误码：** `"1008008"`、`"1008027"`、`"1008028"`、`"40001"`、`"109001"`。

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "sale_no": "MS202610310007",
    "old_expire_date": "2027-10-30",
    "new_expire_date": "2027-11-29",
    "extended_days_total": 30,
    "extendable_days_left": 60,
    "use_no": "MU20261101000044"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 卡必须 `ACTIVE`/`PENDING`/`EXHAUSTED`（`EXPIRED` 需运营特批，走 `basis = 'MANUAL'` + `approval_no`）；2) `extendable = TRUE`；3) `extended_days + extend_days <= max_extend_days`（`"1008029"`）；4) `basis = 'OPTB-PKG-EXT'` 时校验该会员本周期权益未用完（`times_remaining > 0`）；5) `UPDATE extended_days` 与 `effective_expire_date`；6) **写一条 `use_type='EXTEND'`、`use_qty=0` 的核销流水留痕**；7) 若是权益延长，同步递增 `m10_member_benefit.times_used_by_member`；8) 清缓存、写审计。**错误码：** `"1008008"`、`"1008016"`、`"1008024"`、`"1008029"`、`"1008030"`。



#### API-29 批量生成关怀任务

**请求**

```

```

```json
{
  "care_types": ["RECALL_IDLE", "PACKAGE_EXPIRE"],
  "store_id": 101,
  "due_date": "2026-11-01",
  "assignee_id": null,
  "notify_channel": "WECHAT",
  "dry_run": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "batch_no": "MCB20261031001",
    "generated": 42,
    "skipped_duplicate": 6,
    "skipped_no_consent": 3,
    "skipped_closed": 2,
    "by_type": [{"care_type": "RECALL_IDLE", "generated": 28}, {"care_type": "PACKAGE_EXPIRE", "generated": 14}],
    "notify_sent": 42
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按类型分别扫：`RECALL_IDLE` → `idx_m10_member_profile_recall`；`BIRTHDAY` → 生日表达式索引；`CHILD_FOLLOWUP` → `idx_m10_member_profile_child`；`PACKAGE_EXPIRE` → `idx_m10_pkg_sale_expiring`；`PACKAGE_IDLE` → 售出 60 天无核销；`ORTHOK_FOLLOWUP` → 调 U-09；2) 逐条走 `ON CONFLICT (member_no, care_type, due_date) DO NOTHING`（`uk_m10_member_care_dedup`）；3) `care_opt_in = FALSE` → 生成但 `exec_status = 'SKIPPED'`、`notify_result = 'NO_CONSENT'`；4) 档案 `CLOSED` → 跳过；5) 调 U-07 发送消息（异步）；6) `due_date` 不得早于今天；7) 写审计。**错误码：** `"40001"`、`"109001"`。

**业务逻辑：** 1) 任务必须存在且非终态（`"1008033"`/`"1008034"`）；2) `SKIPPED` 必须填 `skip_reason`；3) `converted = TRUE` 必须有 `converted_type`，`ORDER`/`FOLLOWUP`/`PACKAGE` 还需 `converted_ref_no`；4) `contact_count + 1`，超 `CARE_MAX_CONTACT_PER_TASK` → `"1008037"`；5) 更新 `contacted_at`/`convert`/`converted_at`；6) 同步更新 `m10_member_care` 与 `m10_member_stat`（异步重算当日行）；7) 写审计。**错误码：** `"1008033"`、`"1008034"`、`"1008037"`、`"40001"`。

#### API-35 退换冲减回写（内部契约，P-23 的配套）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/members/on-returned
X-Internal-Token: ***
Idempotency-Key: 9e8d7c6b-5a4f-3e2d-1c0b-9a8f7e6d5c4b
```

```json
{
  "sales_order_no": "SO202610310021",
  "refund_no": "RF202611100002",
  "return_no": "RT202611100002",
  "return_type": "PARTIAL",
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "member_no": "M202610200001",
  "returned_product_codes": ["OPLN000456"],
  "returned_charge_codes": [],
  "returned_at": "2026-11-10T14:00:00+08:00",
  "free_residual_policy": "KEEP",
  "operator_id": 6001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "sales_order_no": "SO202610310021",
    "refund_no": "RF202611100002",
    "affected_sales": [
      {"sale_no": "MS202610310007", "sale_status": "ACTIVE", "reversed_cnt": 1,
       "remaining_ratio": 71.43}
    ],
    "reversed": [
      {"use_no": "MU20261110000031", "use_type": "REVERSE", "use_qty": -1.00,
       "reversed_use_id": 88012, "reverse_reason": "PARTIAL_RETURN",
       "free_residual_policy": "KEEP"}
    ],
    "already_reversed": [],
    "is_idempotent": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

1. **参数校验。** `return_type` ∈ {`FULL`, `PARTIAL`}；`refund_no` 非空；`free_residual_policy` ∈ {`KEEP`, `WRITE_OFF`, `COMPENSATE`}。
2. **定位受影响的卡。** 按 `sales_order_no` 查 `m10_member_package_sale`：
   - 无卡（该销售单不含套餐）→ 返回 `affected_sales = []`，`code = 0`（**正常情况，不报错**）；
   - 有卡 → 逐卡处理。
3. **按退换类型决定冲减范围（§3.4.5）：**
   - `FULL` → 该卡全部 `CONSUME` 行逐条冲减；
   - `PARTIAL` → 仅冲减与 `returned_product_codes` / `returned_charge_codes` 匹配的内容项所对应的 `CONSUME` 行。
4. **逐条冲减（每条独立幂等）：**
   - 幂等键 `RETURN:{refund_no}:{sale_no}:{item_code}:{原行.usage_seq}`；
   - `uk_m10_pkg_use_reverse(reversed_use_id)` 防重复冲减；已冲减的进 `already_reversed`；
   - 插入 `use_type='REVERSE'`、`use_qty = -原值`、`reversed_use_id`、`reversed_qty`、`reverse_reason`、`free_residual_policy` 的新行；
   - 依策略决定冗余额度是否加回：`KEEP`/`COMPENSATE` 加回，`WRITE_OFF` 不加回。
5. **卡状态处理。**
   - `FULL` + `KEEP` + 卡未过期 → `sale_status = 'ACTIVE'`（额度归零则 `EXHAUSTED`）；
   - `FULL` → `sale_status = 'RETURNED'`，写 `refund_no` / `returned_at` / `returned_quota`；
   - 卡已 `EXPIRED` → 状态不变（仍 `EXPIRED`），只记流水。
6. **`COMPENSATE` 补偿。** 生成一张新卡（`package_code` 同、`start_date = today`、`valid_days = 剩余天数`、`sales_order_no = 'COMP-' + refund_no`），把冲减的额度写入新卡。
7. **发布事件。** `m10.member.package.reversed` + （若卡状态变为 `RETURNED`）`m10.member.package.returned`。
8. **幂等。** 请求头 `Idempotency-Key` 命中 → 返回上次结果 + `is_idempotent = true`。

**与 `m16.optical.returned` 事件的关系：** 两者共用同一 Service 方法 `MemberPackageService.on_returned()`。事件由 MQ 消费触发，API-35 由 M16-04 同步调用触发。**先到者执行，后到者命中幂等返回。** 若两者同时到达，数据库 `uk_m10_pkg_use_reverse` 保证只有一条 REVERSE 行成功插入。

**性能：** P95 ≤ 200ms；QPS 峰值 3。

**错误码：** `"1008008"`、`"1008027"`、`"1008028"`、`"40001"`。

---
### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/internal/m10/member.py
"""M10-08 视光会员与权益 —— 内部契约路由（P-21 / P-22 / P-23 / on-returned）。

⚠ 路由声明顺序极其重要：
   P-22 的 /members/benefits/by-patient/{patient_id} 必须声明在
   P-21 的 /members/{member_no} 之前，否则 FastAPI 会把 "benefits"
   当成 member_no 匹配掉。同时给 member_no 加路径正则约束做双保险。
"""

from typing import List, Literal, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import success_response
from app.schemas.m10.member import (
    MemberBenefitsOut, MemberProfileDetailOut, PackageUseIn, PackageUseOut,
    PackageReverseIn, PackageReverseOut,
)
from app.services.m10.member_benefit_service import MemberBenefitService
from app.services.m10.member_package_service import MemberPackageService
from app.services.m10.member_profile_service import MemberProfileService

internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/members", tags=["M10-08-内部契约"])

MEMBER_NO_PATTERN = r"^M[0-9]{14}$"


# ==================== P-22（必须声明在 P-21 之前）====================

@internal_router.get(
    "/benefits/by-patient/{patient_id}",
    summary="P-22 视光会员权益与套餐（供 M16-06 优惠试算）",
)
async def internal_member_benefits_by_patient(
    patient_id: int,
    category_codes: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="视光品类，逗号分隔"),
    product_codes: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="商品编码，逗号分隔"),
    charge_codes: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="收费项目编码，逗号分隔"),
    service_actions: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="服务动作，逗号分隔"),
    settlement_base: Optional[float] = Query(
        None, ge=0, description="M16-06 传入的可优惠基数，仅用于门槛比较，不参与运算"),
    use_package: bool = Query(True, description="是否包含套餐权益"),
    include_unavailable: bool = Query(False, description="是否返回不可用权益"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回视光权益参数与套餐可用次数。不产出任何金额。"""
    svc = MemberBenefitService(db)
    result = await svc.get_benefits_by_patient(
        patient_id=patient_id,
        category_codes=_split_csv(category_codes),
        product_codes=_split_csv(product_codes),
        charge_codes=_split_csv(charge_codes),
        service_actions=_split_csv(service_actions),
        settlement_base=settlement_base,
        use_package=use_package,
        include_unavailable=include_unavailable,
    )
    return success_response(result)


# ==================== P-21 ====================

@internal_router.get(
    "/{member_no}",
    summary="P-21 取视光会员档案",
)
async def internal_member_profile(
    member_no: str,
    include_packages: bool = Query(False, description="是否返回可用套餐"),
    include_benefit_params: bool = Query(False, description="是否返回权益参数摘要"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按会员号取视光会员档案（含视光专属属性与等级快照）。"""
    if not _valid_member_no(member_no):
        # 走到这里说明是 /members/benefits 之类的非会员号路径，
        # 交由上层 404 处理，避免把 "benefits" 当会员号查询。
        raise _not_found(member_no)
    svc = MemberProfileService(db)
    result = await svc.get_profile(
        member_no=member_no,
        include_packages=include_packages,
        include_benefit_params=include_benefit_params,
    )
    return success_response(result)


# ==================== P-23 ====================

@internal_router.post(
    "/on-consumed",
    summary="P-23 消费完成回写（套餐核销，幂等）",
)
async def internal_on_consumed(
    payload: PackageUseIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """消费完成回写：套餐核销 + 会员档案视光属性回写。

    幂等键（三层）：
      1. Redis m10:idem:member_use:{key}  NX EX 86400
      2. DB uk_m10_pkg_use_idem(idempotency_key, created_at)
      3. DB uk_m10_pkg_use_occur(sale_id, item_id, usage_seq) WHERE use_type='CONSUME'

    部分成功语义：全部跳过返回 code=0 + results=[]，不让 M16 的取镜流程回滚。
    """
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = MemberPackageService(db)
    result = await svc.on_consumed(payload, idem_key=idem_key)
    return success_response(result)


# ==================== 冲减回写（P-23 配套）====================

@internal_router.post(
    "/on-returned",
    summary="退换冲减回写（订阅 m16.optical.returned 的同步兜底）",
)
async def internal_on_returned(
    payload: PackageReverseIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按整单退 / 部分退 / 已过期三种口径冲减套餐核销。"""
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = MemberPackageService(db)
    result = await svc.on_returned(payload, idem_key=idem_key)
    return success_response(result)


# ==================== 工具函数 ====================

def _split_csv(value: Optional[str]) -> List[str]:
    """把逗号分隔字符串切成列表，去空白与空项。"""
    if not value:
        return []
    return [item.strip() for item in value.split(",") if item.strip()]


def _valid_member_no(value: str) -> bool:
    """会员号格式校验：M + 14 位数字（M03-03 会员号规则）。"""
    import re
    return re.fullmatch(MEMBER_NO_PATTERN, value) is not None


def _not_found(member_no: str):
    """构造资源不存在异常。"""
    from app.core.exceptions import BizException
    return BizException("1008001", "视光会员档案不存在：%s" % member_no)
```

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/member.py
"""M10-08 视光会员与权益 —— 对外路由（精选：档案、套餐核销、关怀）。"""

from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m10.member import (
    CareCreateIn, CareExecuteIn, MemberProfileCreateIn, MemberProfileQuery,
    MemberProfileUpdateIn, PackageCreateIn, PackageSaleCreateIn, PackageUseIn,
)
from app.services.m10.member_care_service import MemberCareService
from app.services.m10.member_package_service import MemberPackageService
from app.services.m10.member_profile_service import MemberProfileService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/members", tags=["M10-08-视光会员"])


@router.get("/profiles", summary="API-02 会员档案分页列表")
async def list_profiles(
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    level_code: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    profile_status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    is_child_myopia: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, ge=0),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:member:view")),
):
    """按关键字/等级/状态/门店筛选视光会员档案。"""
    svc = MemberProfileService(db)
    q = MemberProfileQuery(
        keyword=keyword, level_code=level_code, profile_status=profile_status,
        is_child_myopia=is_child_myopia, store_id=store_id,
        page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    items, total = await svc.list_profiles(q, user=user)
    return success_response(PageResult(
        items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        pages=(total + page_size - 1) // page_size,
    ))


@router.post("/profiles", summary="API-04 开通视光会员档案")
async def create_profile(
    payload: MemberProfileCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:member:create")),
):
    """开通视光档案。会员号与等级由 M03-03 决定，本接口不接收这两个字段。"""
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = MemberProfileService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.create_profile(payload, idem_key=idem_key, operator=user))


@router.put("/profiles/{member_no}", summary="API-05 修改视光专属属性")
async def update_profile(
    member_no: str,
    payload: MemberProfileUpdateIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:member:update")),
):
    """仅允许修改视光专属属性；等级与会员状态由 M03-03 维护。"""
    svc = MemberProfileService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.update_profile(member_no, payload, operator=user))


@router.get("/package-sales/{sale_no}", summary="API-19 套餐售出详情")
async def get_package_sale(
    sale_no: str,
    with_usage: bool = Query(False),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:member:view")),
):
    """返回套餐卡逐项实时额度（权威口径 SUM(use_qty)）。"""
    svc = MemberPackageService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_sale_detail(sale_no, with_usage=with_usage))


@router.post("/package-sales/{sale_no}/use", summary="API-20 套餐核销（幂等）")
async def use_package(
    sale_no: str,
    payload: PackageUseIn,
    request: Request,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:member:use")),
):
    """套餐核销。支持 dry_run 预检；幂等三重防线见 Service 层注释。"""
    idem_key = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")
    svc = MemberPackageService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.use_package(sale_no, payload, idem_key=idem_key, operator=user))


@router.post("/care/generate", summary="API-29 批量生成关怀任务")
async def generate_care(
    payload: CareCreateIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:member:care")),
):
    """按规则批量生成召回/生日/复查/套餐到期任务。"""
    svc = MemberCareService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.generate(payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/care/{care_no}/execute", summary="API-30 执行关怀任务")
async def execute_care(
    care_no: str,
    payload: CareExecuteIn,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:member:care")),
):
    """记录联系/转化/跳过。"""
    svc = MemberCareService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.execute(care_no, payload, operator=user))
```

**路由实现要点：**

| # | 要点 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **P-22 声明在 P-21 之前** | FastAPI 按声明顺序匹配；`/benefits/by-patient/{pid}` 必须早于 `/{member_no}` |
| 2 | **`member_no` 正则双保险** | `_valid_member_no()` 校验 `^M[0-9]{14}$`，非法直接 404，不会把 `benefits` 当会员号查库 |
| 3 | **权限装饰器前置** | 对外接口一律 `require_permission`；内部契约靠 `X-Internal-Token` 网关校验（不在本层） |
| 4 | **幂等键从请求头取** | 统一 `request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key")` 传给 Service，Service 决定是否使用 |
| 5 | **`dry_run` 通过 Pydantic 字段传递** | 避免新增路由，保持 API-20 单一入口 |
| 6 | **无金额字段** | 所有响应 Schema 中不存在金额字段（除带 `_snap`/`_ref` 的展示值 + 说明字段） |

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m10/member_package_service.py
"""M10-08 视光会员与权益 —— 套餐服务（核销 / 冲减 / 售出）。

本模块铁律（写在这里提醒所有维护者）：
  1. 不生成会员号、不改等级、不改成长值 —— 那是 M03-03 / M19 的事
  2. 不计算任何金额 —— 那是 M16-06 / M16-08 的事
  3. 核销流水只增不改 —— 冲减靠新增 REVERSE 行
"""

from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m10.m16_optical_client import M16OpticalClient
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m10.member import (
    M10MemberPackage, M10MemberPackageItem, M10MemberPackageSale,
    M10MemberPackageUse, M10MemberProfile,
)
from app.services.m10.member_code_service import MemberCodeService

IDEM_TTL = 86400
CENT = Decimal("0.01")


class MemberPackageService:
    """套餐售出、核销、冲减的领域服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.code_svc = MemberCodeService(db)
        self.m16 = M16OpticalClient()

    # ==================== 核销（P-23 / API-20 共用）====================

    async def use_package(
        self,
        sale_no: str,
        payload: Any,
        idem_key: Optional[str] = None,
        operator: Any = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """核销单个内容项。dry_run=True 时只校验不落库。"""
        # 1. 幂等：Redis 第一道
        key = self._build_idem_key(
            source_type=payload.source_type,
            source_biz_no=payload.source_biz_no,
            sale_no=sale_no,
            item_code=payload.item_code or payload.service_action or "",
            usage_seq=0,
        )
        if idem_key:
            key = "HEADER:%s:%s" % (idem_key, payload.item_code or payload.service_action)
        hit = await self._redis_idem_probe(key)
        if hit:
            return hit

        # 2. 锁卡，串行化同一张卡的并发核销
        sale = await self._lock_sale(sale_no)

        # 3. 卡状态校验
        if sale.sale_status == "EXPIRED":
            raise BizException("1008030", "套餐卡已过期",
                               data={"sale_no": sale_no, "expire": str(sale.effective_expire_date)})
        if sale.sale_status != "ACTIVE":
            raise BizException("1008024", "套餐卡状态不允许核销",
                               data={"sale_no": sale_no, "sale_status": sale.sale_status})
        if sale.paid_status != "PAID":
            raise BizException("1008024", "套餐卡未付清，不可核销",
                               data={"paid_status": sale.paid_status})

        # 4. 定位内容项（item_code 精确 / service_action 匹配）
        item = await self._resolve_item(sale, payload)
        key = self._build_idem_key(
            source_type=payload.source_type,
            source_biz_no=payload.source_biz_no,
            sale_no=sale_no,
            item_code=item.item_code,
            usage_seq=0,
        )

        # 5. 幂等：DB 第二道（同卡同项同序号由 uk_m10_pkg_use_occur 兜底）
        op_seq = await self._next_usage_seq(sale.id, item.id)
        done = await self._find_by_idem(key, op_seq)
        if done:
            await self._redis_idem_store(key, done)
            done["is_idempotent"] = True
            return done

        # 6. 额度 / 周期 / 冷却校验
        net_used = await self._net_used(sale.id, item.id)
        remaining = self._remaining_of(item, net_used)
        if remaining is not None and remaining < payload.use_qty:
            raise BizException(
                "1008025",
                "剩余额度不足：%s 剩余 %s，本次申请 %s" % (item.item_name, remaining, payload.use_qty),
                data={"sale_no": sale_no, "item_code": item.item_code,
                      "remaining": float(remaining), "requested": float(payload.use_qty)},
            )
        await self._assert_period_limit(sale.id, item, payload.use_qty)
        await self._assert_cooldown(sale.id, item)

        if getattr(payload, "dry_run", False):
            return {
                "dry_run": True, "can_use": True, "max_usable_qty": float(remaining or 0),
                "remaining": float(remaining) if remaining is not None else None,
                "reject_reason": None, "reject_message": None,
            }

        # 7. 落库：先流水，再冗余计数
        now = payload.used_at or datetime.now()
        use_no = await self.code_svc.next("MU", seq_len=6, when=now)
        frozen = self._frozen_quota(item)

        use = M10MemberPackageUse(
            use_no=use_no,
            sale_id=sale.id,
            sale_no=sale.sale_no,
            item_id=item.id,
            item_code=item.item_code,
            item_seq=item.item_seq,
            item_type=item.item_type,
            package_id=sale.package_id,
            package_code=sale.package_code,
            member_no=sale.member_no,
            patient_id=payload.patient_id or sale.patient_id,
            use_type="CONSUME",
            use_qty=payload.use_qty,
            usage_seq=op_seq,
            unit_name=item.unit_name,
            quota_before=frozen,
            quota_after=(frozen - payload.use_qty) if frozen is not None else None,
            source_type=payload.source_type,
            source_biz_no=payload.source_biz_no,
            source_line_no=getattr(payload, "source_line_no", None),
            sales_order_no=getattr(payload, "sales_order_no", None),
            idempotency_key=key,
            idempotency_scope="HEADER" if idem_key else "COMPUTED",
            used_at=now,
            operator_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            operator_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            store_id=sale.store_id,
            remark=getattr(payload, "remark", None),
        )
        self.db.add(use)

        # 8. 冗余计数（事务内同步，每日 02:10 再对账）
        remaining_after = await self._apply_quota_delta(sale, item, payload.use_qty)
        result = self._to_use_out(use, sale, remaining_after)

        await self.db.commit()

        # 9. 提交后：缓存、事件、档案回写
        await self._redis_idem_store(key, result)
        await self._invalidate_cache(sale.member_no, sale.patient_id)
        await self._after_consumed(sale, item, payload)
        return result

    async def on_consumed(self, payload: Any, idem_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-23 消费完成回写：逐项尽力核销，部分成功不报错。

        返回结构包含 results（成功项）、skipped（跳过项）、档案回写结果。
        """
        member_no = payload.member_no
        if not member_no:
            member_no = await self._resolve_member_no(payload.patient_id)
        profile = await self._get_or_create_profile(
            member_no, payload.patient_id,
            auto_create=getattr(payload, "auto_create_profile", True),
        )

        results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        skipped: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for line in payload.items:
            sale = await self._match_sale(
                member_no=member_no,
                package_sale_no=getattr(line, "package_sale_no", None),
                item_code=getattr(line, "item_code", None),
                service_action=getattr(line, "service_action", None),
            )
            if sale is None:
                skipped.append({
                    "item_code": getattr(line, "item_code", None),
                    "service_action": getattr(line, "service_action", None),
                    "reason": "NO_MATCHING_PACKAGE_ITEM",
                })
                continue
            try:
                one = await self.use_package(
                    sale.sale_no,
                    _line_payload(payload, line),
                    idem_key=idem_key,
                    operator=None,
                )
                results.append(one)
            except BizException as exc:
                # 单行失败不影响其它行，也不影响 M16 的取镜流程
                skipped.append({
                    "item_code": getattr(line, "item_code", None),
                    "service_action": getattr(line, "service_action", None),
                    "reason": exc.code,
                    "message": exc.message,
                })

        if profile and payload.source_type == "ORDER_DELIVER":
            await self._touch_fit_profile(profile, payload)
            await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "member_no": member_no,
            "source_type": payload.source_type,
            "source_biz_no": payload.source_biz_no,
            "results": results,
            "skipped": skipped,
            "profile_created": bool(profile and profile.id is None),
            "profile_updated": payload.source_type == "ORDER_DELIVER",
        }

    # ==================== 冲减（API-23 / API-35 / 事件共用）====================

    async def on_returned(self, payload: Any, idem_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """按整单退 / 部分退 / 已过期三种口径冲减。

        幂等：
          - 幂等键 RETURN:{refund_no}:{sale_no}:{item_code}:{usage_seq}
          - uk_m10_pkg_use_reverse(reversed_use_id) 保证一条原行只冲减一次
        """
        sales = await self._lock_sales_by_order(payload.sales_order_no)
        affected: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        reversed_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        already: List[str] = []

        for sale in sales:
            # 决定冲减范围
            if payload.return_type == "FULL":
                targets = await self._full_consume_lines(sale.id)
            else:
                targets = await self._partial_consume_lines(
                    sale.id,
                    product_codes=getattr(payload, "returned_product_codes", None) or [],
                    charge_codes=getattr(payload, "returned_charge_codes", None) or [],
                )

            for origin in targets:
                done = await self._find_reverse(origin.id)
                if done:
                    already.append(origin.use_no)
                    continue
                row = await self._insert_reverse(
                    sale, origin,
                    refund_no=payload.refund_no,
                    reason=payload.return_reason,
                    policy=payload.free_residual_policy,
                )
                reversed_rows.append(row)
                # KEEP / COMPENSATE 才把额度加回
                if payload.free_residual_policy in ("KEEP", "COMPENSATE"):
                    await self._apply_quota_delta(sale, origin, -origin.use_qty, is_reverse=True)

            await self._settle_sale_after_return(sale, payload, reversed_rows)
            affected.append({
                "sale_no": sale.sale_no,
                "sale_status": sale.sale_status,
                "reversed_cnt": len(reversed_rows),
                "remaining_ratio": float(sale.remaining_ratio),
            })

        self.db.add(_audit_log("PACKAGE_RETURN", payload))
        await self.db.commit()
        await self._invalidate_cache(None, payload.patient_id)
        return {
            "sales_order_no": payload.sales_order_no,
            "refund_no": payload.refund_no,
            "affected_sales": affected,
            "reversed": reversed_rows,
            "already_reversed": already,
            "is_idempotent": bool(idem_key) and not reversed_rows,
        }

    # ==================== 内部工具 ====================

    @staticmethod
    def _build_idem_key(
        source_type: str, source_biz_no: str, sale_no: str,
        item_code: str, usage_seq: int,
    ) -> str:
        """幂等键：{source_type}:{source_biz_no}:{sale_no}:{item_code}:{usage_seq}。"""
        return "%s:%s:%s:%s:%s" % (source_type, source_biz_no, sale_no, item_code, usage_seq)

    @staticmethod
    def _remaining_of(item: M10MemberPackageItem, net_used: Decimal) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """权威剩余额度。None 表示不限次。"""
        if item.item_type == "TIMES":
            if item.total_times is None:
                return None
            return Decimal(item.total_times) - net_used
        if item.item_type == "GOODS":
            if item.total_qty is None:
                return None
            return Decimal(item.total_qty) - net_used
        # SERVICE：total_times 为空即不限次
        if item.total_times is None:
            return None
        return Decimal(item.total_times) - net_used

    @staticmethod
    def _frozen_quota(item: M10MemberPackageItem) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """把冗余计数换算成统一额度值，用于写入 quota_before。"""
        if item.item_type == "TIMES":
            if item.total_times is None:
                return None
            return Decimal(item.total_times - item.used_times)
        if item.item_type == "GOODS":
            if item.total_qty is None:
                return None
            return Decimal(item.total_qty) - Decimal(item.used_qty)
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def calc_remaining_ratio(total_quota: Decimal, used_quota: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        """剩余额度百分比：ROUND_HALF_UP 保留 2 位（口径见 3.4.2）。"""
        if total_quota <= 0:
            return Decimal("100.00")
        ratio = (total_quota - used_quota) / total_quota * Decimal("100")
        value = ratio.quantize(CENT, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        if value < 0:
            return Decimal("0.00")
        if value > 100:
            return Decimal("100.00")
        return value

    async def _assert_period_limit(self, sale_id: int, item: M10MemberPackageItem,
                                   qty: Decimal) -> None:
        """校验周期领取上限（护理液季度 3 瓶等）。违反抛 1008022。"""
        if item.period_type == "NONE" or item.period_limit_qty is None:
            return
        truncated = await self._period_used(sale_id, item.item_code, item.period_type)
        if truncated + qty > Decimal(item.period_limit_qty):
            raise BizException(
                "1008022",
                "超出周期领取上限：本周期已领 %s，上限 %s，本次申请 %s"
                % (truncated, item.period_limit_qty, qty),
                data={"period_type": item.period_type,
                      "period_used": float(truncated),
                      "period_limit": float(item.period_limit_qty)},
            )

    async def _assert_cooldown(self, sale_id: int, item: M10MemberPackageItem) -> None:
        """校验冷却期（OK 镜参数复测 90 天等）。违反抛 1008023。"""
        if not item.cooldown_days:
            return
        last_used = await self._last_used_at(sale_id, item.item_code)
        if last_used is None:
            return
        until = last_used.date() + timedelta(days=item.cooldown_days)
        if date.today() < until:
            raise BizException(
                "1008023",
                "冷却期内，最早 %s 可核销" % until.isoformat(),
                data={"cooldown_until": until.isoformat()},
            )

    # 以下方法为仓储层细节，签名固定，便于替换实现
    async def _lock_sale(self, sale_no: str) -> M10MemberPackageSale:
        """SELECT ... FOR UPDATE 锁卡。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _resolve_item(self, sale: M10MemberPackageSale, payload: Any) -> M10MemberPackageItem:
        """按 item_code 或 service_action 定位内容项。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _next_usage_seq(self, sale_id: int, item_id: int) -> int:
        """同一卡同一项的下一个核销序号。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _net_used(self, sale_id: int, item_id: int) -> Decimal:
        """权威已用额度 = SUM(use_qty)（含 REVERSE 与 ADJUST）。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _period_used(self, sale_id: int, item_code: str, period: str) -> Decimal:
        """本周期已用量。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _last_used_at(self, sale_id: int, item_code: str) -> Optional[datetime]:
        """最近一次 CONSUME 时间，用于冷却期判定。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _apply_quota_delta(self, sale: M10MemberPackageSale,
                                 item: M10MemberPackageItem,
                                 delta: Decimal, is_reverse: bool = False) -> Decimal:
        """同步冗余计数并返回最新剩余额度。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _find_by_idem(self, key: str, usage_seq: int) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """按幂等键或 (sale_id, item_id, usage_seq) 查已有流水。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _find_reverse(self, origin_use_id: int) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """查某条原核销行是否已被冲减。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _insert_reverse(self, sale: M10MemberPackageSale,
                              origin: M10MemberPackageUse,
                              refund_no: str, reason: str,
                              policy: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """插入一条 REVERSE 行。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _after_consumed(self, sale: M10MemberPackageSale,
                              item: M10MemberPackageItem, payload: Any) -> None:
        """核销成功后的副作用：事件发布、统计刷新。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _redis_idem_probe(self, key: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """幂等键探测（第一道防线）。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _redis_idem_store(self, key: str, value: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """幂等键落 Redis（TTL = M10.MEMBER.PACKAGE_USE_IDEM_TTL）。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _invalidate_cache(self, member_no: Optional[str],
                                patient_id: Optional[int]) -> None:
        """失效会员权益与档案缓存。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _resolve_member_no(self, patient_id: int) -> str:
        """按患者反查会员号（调 U-01）。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _get_or_create_profile(self, member_no: str, patient_id: int,
                                     auto_create: bool) -> Optional[M10MemberProfile]:
        """取视光档案，必要时自动创建。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _match_sale(self, member_no: str, package_sale_no: Optional[str],
                          item_code: Optional[str],
                          service_action: Optional[str]) -> Optional[M10MemberPackageSale]:
        """在会员的 ACTIVE 卡中匹配目标卡（按到期日升序取第一张）。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _touch_fit_profile(self, profile: M10MemberProfile, payload: Any) -> None:
        """回写首次/最近配镜日期与累计次数。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _lock_sales_by_order(self, sales_order_no: str) -> List[M10MemberPackageSale]:
        """锁定某销售单关联的全部套餐卡。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _full_consume_lines(self, sale_id: int) -> List[M10MemberPackageUse]:
        """整单退：取该卡全部 CONSUME 行。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _partial_consume_lines(self, sale_id: int, product_codes: List[str],
                                     charge_codes: List[str]) -> List[M10MemberPackageUse]:
        """部分退：取与退货商品匹配的 CONSUME 行。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _settle_sale_after_return(self, sale: M10MemberPackageSale,
                                        payload: Any,
                                        reversed_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        """退换后的卡状态流转。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def _to_use_out(self, use: M10MemberPackageUse,
                    sale: M10MemberPackageSale,
                    remaining_after: Decimal) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """组装核销响应体。"""
        return {
            "use_no": use.use_no,
            "sale_no": use.sale_no,
            "item_code": use.item_code,
            "item_type": use.item_type,
            "use_type": use.use_type,
            "use_qty": float(use.use_qty),
            "usage_seq": use.usage_seq,
            "quota_before": float(use.quota_before) if use.quota_before is not None else None,
            "quota_after": float(remaining_after),
            "sale_status": sale.sale_status,
            "is_idempotent": False,
            "used_at": use.used_at.isoformat(),
        }


class _line_payload:
    """把 P-23 的单行明细包装成 use_package 所需的结构。"""

    def __init__(self, header: Any, line: Any) -> None:
        self.source_type = header.source_type
        self.source_biz_no = header.source_biz_no
        self.sales_order_no = getattr(header, "sales_order_no", None)
        self.patient_id = getattr(line, "patient_id", None) or header.patient_id
        self.item_code = getattr(line, "item_code", None)
        self.service_action = getattr(line, "service_action", None)
        self.use_qty = getattr(line, "use_qty", Decimal("1.00"))
        self.used_at = getattr(header, "used_at", None)
        self.remark = getattr(header, "remark", None)
        self.dry_run = False


def _audit_log(action: str, payload: Any) -> Any:
    """构造审计记录（写 M02-06 sys_audit_log 的本地队列对象）。"""
    raise NotImplementedError
```

```python
# app/services/m10/member_benefit_service.py
"""M10-08 视光会员与权益 —— 权益参数服务（P-22 的核心实现）。

⚠ 本文件是"不产出金额"这条铁律的执行点。
   任何人在此文件里写 `payable = base * ratio` 都是严重违规。
   本服务只做三件事：取值、过滤、组装参数。
"""

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m10.member import (
    M10MemberBenefit, M10MemberLevelSnap, M10MemberPackageSale, M10MemberProfile,
)


class MemberBenefitService:
    """视光权益参数组装（P-22）。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db

    async def get_benefits_by_patient(
        self,
        patient_id: int,
        category_codes: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        product_codes: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        charge_codes: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        service_actions: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        settlement_base: Optional[float] = None,
        use_package: bool = True,
        include_unavailable: bool = False,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """返回视光权益参数与可用套餐。响应中不含任何金额字段。"""
        profile = await self._get_profile_by_patient(patient_id)
        if profile is None:
            # 非会员是正常业务状态，不是错误
            return self._empty_result(patient_id)

        snap = await self._get_current_snap(patient_id)
        if snap is None or self._is_too_stale(snap):
            raise BizException(
                "1008007", "等级快照陈旧，无法提供视光权益参数",
                data={"patient_id": patient_id, "is_stale": True,
                      "synced_at": snap.synced_at.isoformat() if snap else None},
            )

        benefits_available = (
            profile.profile_status == "ACTIVE" and profile.member_status == "ACTIVE"
        )

        # 缓存键包含 benefit_digest：权益一变，键即变，天然失效
        cache_key = "m10:member:benefit:%s:%s:%s" % (
            patient_id, snap.benefit_digest, int(use_package))
        cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
        if cached:
            return json.loads(cached)

        benefits: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        if benefits_available:
            benefits = await self._collect_benefits(
                profile, snap,
                category_codes=category_codes or [],
                service_actions=service_actions or [],
                include_unavailable=include_unavailable,
            )

        packages = []
        package_params = []
        if use_package:
            packages, package_params = await self._collect_packages(profile)

        result = {
            "patient_id": patient_id,
            "member_no": profile.member_no,
            "is_member": True,
            "profile_status": profile.profile_status,
            "member_status": profile.member_status,
            "benefits_available": benefits_available,
            "level": {
                "member_no": snap.member_no,
                "level_code": snap.level_code,
                "level_name": snap.level_name,
                "level_seq": snap.level_seq,
                "snap_version": snap.snap_version,
                "optical_discount_ratio": _f(snap.optical_discount_ratio),
                "service_discount_ratio": _f(snap.service_discount_ratio),
                "points_rate": _f(snap.points_rate),
                "storage_discount_ratio": _f(snap.storage_discount_ratio),
                "free_service_codes": snap.free_service_codes or [],
                "is_stale": snap.is_stale,
                "synced_at": snap.synced_at.isoformat(),
            },
            "benefits": benefits,
            "packages": packages,
            "package_params": package_params,
            "degraded": snap.is_stale,
            "degraded_reason": "M03_SNAPSHOT_STALE" if snap.is_stale else None,
            "calc_owner": "M16-06",
            "calc_note": ("本响应只提供权益参数，不包含任何金额；"
                          "实收金额由 M16-06 POST /internal/v1/m16/discount/calc 计算"),
        }

        await redis_client.setex(
            cache_key,
            int(settings.M10_MEMBER_BENEFIT_CACHE_TTL),
            json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, default=str),
        )
        return result

    async def _collect_benefits(
        self,
        profile: M10MemberProfile,
        snap: M10MemberLevelSnap,
        category_codes: List[str],
        service_actions: List[str],
        include_unavailable: bool,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """收集启用的视光专属权益，并做四类硬性过滤。"""
        stmt = (
            select(M10MemberBenefit)
            .where(
                M10MemberBenefit.is_enabled.is_(True),
                M10MemberBenefit.is_deleted.is_(False),
                M10MemberBenefit.effective_from <= date.today(),
                (M10MemberBenefit.effective_to.is_(None))
                | (M10MemberBenefit.effective_to >= date.today()),
                M10MemberBenefit.min_level_seq <= snap.level_seq,
            )
            .order_by(M10MemberBenefit.stack_priority, M10MemberBenefit.sort_no)
        )
        rows = (await self.db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()

        out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for b in rows:
            # 过滤 1：儿童防控专属
            if b.require_child_myopia and not profile.is_child_myopia:
                continue
            # 过滤 2：OK 镜戴镜专属
            if b.require_orthok and not profile.is_orthok_wearer:
                continue
            # 过滤 3：品类范围（请求指定时）
            if category_codes and b.scope_kind not in ("ALL", "OPTICAL"):
                if b.scope_kind not in category_codes:
                    continue
            # 过滤 4：服务动作（请求指定时）
            if service_actions and b.benefit_type == "FREE_SERVICE":
                actions = [x.strip() for x in (b.scope_value or "").split(",") if x.strip()]
                if actions and not set(actions) & set(service_actions):
                    continue

            remaining = self._times_remaining(b)
            available = remaining is None or remaining > 0
            if not available and not include_unavailable:
                continue
            out.append({
                "benefit_code": b.benefit_code,
                "benefit_name": b.benefit_name,
                "benefit_type": b.benefit_type,
                "scope_kind": b.scope_kind,
                "scope_value": b.scope_value,
                "ratio": _f(b.ratio_value),
                "times_limit": b.times_limit,
                "times_used": b.times_used_by_member,
                "times_remaining": remaining,
                "extend_days": b.extend_days,
                "is_stackable": b.is_stackable,
                "stack_priority": b.stack_priority,
                "available": available,
                "unavailable_reason": None if available else "本周期次数已用完",
                "description": b.description,
            })
        return out

    async def _collect_packages(
        self, profile: M10MemberProfile
    ) -> "tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, Any]]]":
        """收集可用套餐与逐项实时额度。"""
        stmt = (
            select(M10MemberPackageSale)
            .where(
                M10MemberPackageSale.member_no == profile.member_no,
                M10MemberPackageSale.sale_status == "ACTIVE",
                M10MemberPackageSale.paid_status == "PAID",
                M10MemberPackageSale.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
            .order_by(M10MemberPackageSale.effective_expire_date.asc().nulls_last())
        )
        sales = (await self.db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()

        packages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        params: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for sale in sales:
            if sale.effective_expire_date and sale.effective_expire_date < date.today():
                continue
            items = await self._items_with_remaining(sale)
            packages.append({
                "sale_no": sale.sale_no,
                "member_no": sale.member_no,
                "package_code": sale.package_code,
                "package_name": sale.package_name,
                "package_version": sale.package_version,
                "sale_status": sale.sale_status,
                "paid_status": sale.paid_status,
                "start_date": sale.start_date.isoformat(),
                "effective_expire_date": (
                    sale.effective_expire_date.isoformat()
                    if sale.effective_expire_date else None),
                "total_quota": _f(sale.total_quota),
                "used_quota": _f(sale.used_quota),
                "remaining_ratio": _f(sale.remaining_ratio),
                "is_stackable": False,
                "stackable_level": "NONE",
                "items": items,
            })
            params.append({
                "sale_no": sale.sale_no,
                "package_extendable_days": await self._extendable_days(sale),
                "free_service_actions": [
                    it["service_action"] for it in items
                    if it["item_type"] == "SERVICE" and it.get("service_action")
                ],
                "discount_applicable": False,
                "note": "套餐卡本身不参与折扣叠加，仅其内容项可用",
            })
        return packages, params

    # ---------- 工具方法 ----------

    @staticmethod
    def _times_remaining(b: M10MemberBenefit) -> Optional[int]:
        """剩余次数。None 表示不限次。"""
        if b.times_limit is None:
            return None
        return max(b.times_limit - b.times_used_by_member, 0)

    @staticmethod
    def _is_too_stale(snap: M10MemberLevelSnap) -> bool:
        """快照陈旧是否超过阈值（分钟）。"""
        if not snap.is_stale:
            return False
        minutes = (datetime.now() - snap.synced_at).total_seconds() / 60.0
        return minutes > settings.M10_MEMBER_LEVEL_SNAP_MAX_STALE_MIN

    @staticmethod
    def _empty_result(patient_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """非会员返回空权益（正常业务状态）。"""
        return {
            "patient_id": patient_id, "member_no": None, "is_member": False,
            "profile_status": None, "member_status": None,
            "benefits_available": False, "level": None,
            "benefits": [], "packages": [], "package_params": [],
            "degraded": False, "degraded_reason": None,
            "calc_owner": "M16-06",
            "calc_note": "非视光会员，M16-06 应按普通客户试算",
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _digest(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """权益参数摘要（SHA-256），用于缓存键与幂等比对。"""
        raw = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, default=str)
        return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

    async def _get_profile_by_patient(self, patient_id: int) -> Optional[M10MemberProfile]:
        """按患者查视光档案。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _get_current_snap(self, patient_id: int) -> Optional[M10MemberLevelSnap]:
        """取当前等级快照。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _items_with_remaining(self, sale: M10MemberPackageSale) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """逐项计算实时额度（权威口径 SUM(use_qty)）。"""
        raise NotImplementedError

    async def _extendable_days(self, sale: M10MemberPackageSale) -> int:
        """本卡还可延长天数。"""
        raise NotImplementedError


def _f(value: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[float]:
    """Decimal 转 float 供 JSON 序列化；None 原样返回。"""
    return float(value) if value is not None else None
```

**Service 层设计要点：**

| # | 要点 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **核销执行顺序不可调换** | Redis 幂等 → 锁卡 → 状态校验 → 定位项 → 序号 → DB 幂等 → 额度/周期/冷却 → 落库 → 提交 → 缓存/事件。任何一步提前或滞后都会造成重复核销或脏数据 |
| 2 | **`FOR UPDATE` 在幂等检查之前** | 若先查幂等再锁卡，两个并发请求可能都查不到再各自插入。先锁卡使整个判定-写入过程串行 |
| 3 | **`_remaining_of()` 是唯一权威口径** | 所有剩余额度计算必须调用它（基于 `SUM(use_qty)`），**禁止直接用 `item.used_times` 判断** |
| 4 | **`calc_remaining_ratio()` 单独抽成静态方法** | 便于单元测试覆盖边界（0 总量、负数、超 100） |
| 5 | **`on_consumed` 用 try/except 逐行吞异常** | 单行失败进 `skipped`，不影响其它行，也不让 M16 的取镜流程回滚 |
| 6 | **`on_returned` 的幂等靠 DB 唯一索引** | `_find_reverse()` 先查再插，并发下由 `uk_m10_pkg_use_reverse` 兜底，捕获冲突后归入 `already` |
| 7 | **`MemberBenefitService` 无任何金额运算** | 整个类只做「取值 → 过滤 → 组装」，`settlement_base` 仅作为门槛参数透传，不参与运算 |
| 8 | **`_digest()` 用作缓存键一部分** | 权益参数一变 `benefit_digest` 即变，缓存自然失效，无需手动清理（但等级变更仍会主动删） |
| 9 | **仓储方法声明为 `NotImplementedError`** | 文档示例聚焦业务逻辑；真实实现按仓储模式拆到 `app/repositories/m10/`，签名固定 |

---
## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

Redis Key 统一在本模块域 `m10:member:*` 内（共享规范 §14）。

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10:member:profile:{member_no}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 视光会员档案轻量视图（P-21 热数据） | 档案更新、等级事件、套餐核销 | 6,182 × 1.2KB ≈ 7.4MB |
| `m10:member:profile:patient:{patient_id}` | String | 300s | 患者 → 会员号映射 | 同上 | 6,182 × 40B ≈ 247KB |
| `m10:member:benefit:{patient_id}:{digest}` | String(JSON) | 300s | **P-22 权益参数缓存（核心）** | 等级变更、权益配置变更、快照重建 | 峰值 1,500 × 6KB ≈ 9MB |
| `m10:member:package:active:{member_no}` | String(JSON) | 180s | 会员可用套餐列表 | 套餐核销、售出、退换、延长 | 1,200 × 3KB ≈ 3.6MB |
| `m10:member:package:quota:{sale_no}` | Hash | 180s | 套餐卡逐项剩余额度 | 每次核销/冲减后主动更新 | 900 × 500B ≈ 450KB |
| `m10:member:config` | String(JSON) | 1800s | 13 项参数（P10） | 参数保存后 `DEL` | 1 × 2KB |
| `m10:member:level:map` | String(JSON) | 1800s | 等级 → 序号/名称映射（M03 投影） | 等级定义变更事件 | 1 × 1KB |
| `m10:member:stat:{store_id}:{stat_date}:{level}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 经营统计卡片 | 统计任务重算后 `DEL` | 120 × 2KB ≈ 240KB |
| `m10:member:workbench:{store}:{date}:{user}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 工作台聚合结果 | 自然过期（短 TTL 容忍陈旧） | 30 × 4KB ≈ 120KB |
| `m10:member:care:todo:{assignee_id}` | String | 120s | 我的待办数量 | 任务变更 | 30 × 40B |
| `m10:member:product_status:{code}` | String | 600s | 商品可售状态（M01-08 同步） | `mdm.optical_product.*` 事件 | 500 × 20B |
| `m10:idem:member_profile:{key}` | String | 86400s | 建档幂等键 | 自然过期 | 峰值 200 × 100B |
| `m10:idem:member_use:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | **核销幂等键（核心）** | 自然过期（TTL 可配） | 峰值 3,000 × 600B ≈ 1.8MB |
| `m10:lock:member_package:{sale_no}` | String | 10s | 卡片级分布式锁（辅助 `FOR UPDATE`） | 释放或超时 | 峰值 50 × 40B |
| `m10:lock:member_care_generate` | String | 600s | 任务批量生成互斥 | 释放或超时 | 1 |
| `m10:member:seq:{prefix}:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 172800s | 单号流水计数器 | 自然过期 | 3 × 40B |
| `m10:member:stats:daily` | String(JSON) | 300s | 日报聚合 | 统计任务 | 1 × 4KB |

**缓存总容量估算：** 约 22MB（Redis 实例配置 512MB，占用率 4.3%，安全）。

**缓存击穿、穿透、雪崩的防护：**

| 风险 | 场景 | 防护 |
|---|---|---|
| **穿透** | 大量查询不存在的 `member_no`（恶意或脏数据） | 查不到也缓存空结果 60 秒（`{"__null__": true}`）；`m10:member:profile:*` 键加随机后缀防止空值被刷新 |
| **击穿** | P-22 的某个热门 `benefit_digest` 缓存同时过期 | 缓存重建用 Redis `SET NX` 分布式锁 `m10:lock:member_benefit:{digest}`，其余请求等 200ms 后读缓存 |
| **雪崩** | 大面积缓存同时失效（如权益参数批量变更） | TTL 加 ±10% 随机抖动；批量变更走「异步逐个重建 + 分批失效」，每批 100 个键间隔 50ms |
| **一致性** | 等级变更后缓存仍是旧权益 | 订阅 `m03.member.level_changed` 时**主动 `DEL`** `m10:member:benefit:{patient_id}:*`（用 SCAN 匹配删除，不用 KEYS） |

**为什么 P-22 的缓存 TTL 只有 300 秒？**
因为权益参数直接决定「顾客少付多少钱」。若缓存 30 分钟，运营改了折扣率后，前 30 分钟进来的顾客仍按旧折扣算。300 秒是「能挡住收银峰值 QPS」与「变更 5 分钟内生效」的平衡点。对比 M03-03 的权益查询缓存也是 5 分钟，保持一致。

**缓存与数据库的一致性策略：**

```
写操作（核销/冲减/售出/退换/等级变更）
    │
    ├── 1. 先写 DB（事务提交）
    ├── 2. 提交后再 DEL 缓存（不是先删后写）
    └── 3. 若 DEL 失败 → 投递 MQ 延迟 1 秒重试（最多 3 次）

读操作（P-21 / P-22）
    │
    ├── 1. 读缓存命中 → 返回
    └── 2. 未命中 → 重建 → SETEX
```

> **为什么是「先提交再删缓存」而不是「先删再写」？** 若先删缓存再写 DB，在删除到提交之间有一个窗口期，另一个请求会读不到缓存、从 DB 读到旧值、写回缓存，导致缓存与 DB 长期不一致（经典 Cache-Aside 的并发问题）。「先提交再删」把窗口压缩到提交后的几毫秒内，且即使出现也是短期的旧值，下一次 TTL 到期自动修复。

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

```python
# app/tasks/m10/member_tasks.py
"""M10-08 视光会员与权益 —— Celery 异步任务。"""

import asyncio

from celery import shared_task


@shared_task(name="m10.member.rebuild_benefit_snapshot")
def rebuild_benefit_snapshot_task(member_nos=None, level_code=None):
    """权益参数变更后批量重建会员权益快照。

    参数二选一：指定 member_nos 精确重建；或按 level_code 批量重建。
    """
    return asyncio.run(_rebuild_benefit_snapshot(member_nos, level_code))


async def _rebuild_benefit_snapshot(member_nos, level_code):
    """逐个重建快照，每 100 个批量提交并清缓存。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.member_profile_service import MemberProfileService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = MemberProfileService(db)
        targets = member_nos or await svc.list_member_nos_by_level(level_code)
        ok, failed = 0, 0
        for idx, member_no in enumerate(targets, 1):
            try:
                await svc.refresh_level_snapshot(member_no, source="PULL")
                ok += 1
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                failed += 1
            if idx % 100 == 0:
                await db.commit()
        await db.commit()
        return {"total": len(targets), "ok": ok, "failed": failed}


@shared_task(name="m10.member.notify_care")
def notify_care_task(care_no, channel=None):
    """发送关怀消息（调 M03-05 POST /internal/v1/message/send）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_notify_care(care_no, channel))


async def _notify_care(care_no, channel):
    """发送消息并回写 notify_result。失败进重试队列，不抛异常。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.member_care_service import MemberCareService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = MemberCareService(db)
        return await svc.send_notification(care_no, channel=channel)


@shared_task(name="m10.member.publish_package_used")
def publish_package_used_task(use_no):
    """发布 m10.member.package.used 事件（提交后异步发送，避免事务内发消息）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_publish_package_used(use_no))


async def _publish_package_used(use_no):
    """按核销流水号查详情并投递 MQ。"""
    from app.core.mq import mq_publisher
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.member_package_service import MemberPackageService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = MemberPackageService(db)
        payload = await svc.build_event_payload(use_no)
        await mq_publisher.publish("m10.member.package.used", payload)
        return payload


@shared_task(name="m10.member.reconcile_amount_snapshot", bind=True, max_retries=3)
def reconcile_amount_snapshot_task(self, batch_size=500):
    """每日对账：把 M16-04 的累计配镜金额回填到档案只读镜像。

    差异 > 0.01 元写告警日志并记入对账表。
    """
    try:
        return asyncio.run(_reconcile_amount(batch_size))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=300)


async def _reconcile_amount(batch_size):
    """批量拉取 M16-04 汇总值并与本地镜像比对。"""
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.member_profile_service import MemberProfileService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = MemberProfileService(db)
        return await svc.reconcile_amount_snapshot(batch_size=batch_size)


@shared_task(name="m10.member.retry_idem_cleanup")
def retry_idem_cleanup_task():
    """清理过期的核销幂等键（仅清理已落库已确认的键，防误删）。"""
    return asyncio.run(_cleanup_idem())


async def _cleanup_idem():
    """SCAN 幂等键并清理超过 TTL 2 倍的残留。"""
    from app.core.redis import redis_client

    cursor = 0
    removed = 0
    while True:
        cursor, keys = await redis_client.scan(
            cursor=cursor, match="m10:idem:member_use:*", count=500)
        if keys:
            ttl_map = await redis_client.ttl_many(keys)
            stale = [k for k, ttl in ttl_map.items() if ttl < 0]
            if stale:
                removed += await redis_client.delete(*stale)
        if cursor == 0:
            break
    return {"removed": removed}
```

**Celery 任务清单：**

| 任务名 | 触发方式 | 用途 | 超时 | 重试策略 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10.member.rebuild_benefit_snapshot` | 权益变更 / 等级事件后 | 批量重建权益快照 | 600s | 3 次，退避 60/300/900s |
| `m10.member.notify_care` | 任务生成后 | 发送关怀消息 | 30s | 3 次，退避 30/120/300s |
| `m10.member.publish_package_used` | 核销提交后 | 发布核销事件 | 15s | 5 次，退避 5s |
| `m10.member.reconcile_amount_snapshot` | 每日 01:20 | 金额镜像对账 | 900s | 3 次，退避 300s |
| `m10.member.retry_idem_cleanup` | 每日 04:00 | 清理幂等键残留 | 120s | 不重试 |
| `m10.member.export` | 用户点击导出 | 异步导出 Excel | 1800s | 不重试（用户可重发） |
| `m10.member.print_batch` | 用户点击批量打印 | 生成打印任务 | 600s | 2 次 |

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务名 | Cron | 说明 | 幂等/去重 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job_member_recall_scan` | `0 9 * * *` | 久未配镜召回扫描（`last_fit_date < today − RECALL_IDLE_DAYS`） | `uk_m10_member_care_dedup` |
| `job_member_birthday_scan` | `10 9 * * *` | 生日关怀扫描（生日表达式索引） | 同上 |
| `job_member_child_followup_scan` | `20 9 * * *` | 儿童复查提醒扫描 | 同上 |
| `job_member_package_expire_scan` | `30 9 * * *` | 套餐到期提醒（到期前 `PACKAGE_EXPIRE_REMIND_DAYS`） | 同上 |
| `job_member_package_idle_scan` | `40 9 * * *` | 套餐未核销提醒（售出 60 天无核销） | 同上 |
| `job_member_orthok_followup_scan` | `50 9 * * *` | OK 镜随访提醒（调 U-09） | 同上 |
| `job_member_package_activate` | `0 0 * * *` | `PENDING` 卡到达生效日转 `ACTIVE` | 状态机保证 |
| `job_member_package_expire` | `5 0 * * *` | `ACTIVE` 卡超过有效期转 `EXPIRED` | 状态机保证 |
| `job_member_care_expire` | `20 0 * * *` | `due_date + 30 天` 未处理的任务转 `EXPIRED` | 状态机保证 |
| `job_member_snapshot_refresh` | `0 * * * *` | 每小时拉取陈旧快照并修复 | 由 `is_stale` 判断 |
| `job_member_stat_daily` | `50 0 * * *` | 计算昨日经营统计（`DAY` 粒度） | `uk_m10_member_stat_dim` |
| `job_member_stat_monthly` | `20 1 2 * *` | 计算上月经营统计（`MONTH` 粒度） | 同上 |
| `job_member_amount_reconcile` | `20 1 * * *` | 金额镜像对账 | — |
| `job_member_quota_reconcile` | `10 2 * * *` | 核销冗余计数与流水对账 | 只告警不改历史 |
| `job_member_usage_backfill` | `0 2 * * *` | 已交付订单无核销流水的补偿 | `uk_m10_pkg_use_occur` |
| `job_member_code_reconcile` | `0 3 * * *` | 单号计数器与数据库最大值对账 | — |
| `job_member_refs_reconcile` | `30 1 * * *` | 悬挂引用检查（会员/患者/商品） | 只告警 |
| `job_member_use_partition` | `0 1 1 * *` | 预建核销表下月分区 + 归档超期分区 | `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` |

```python
# app/jobs/m10/member_jobs.py
"""M10-08 视光会员与权益 —— APScheduler 定时任务。"""

import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


async def job_member_quota_reconcile() -> dict:
    """每日 02:10：核销冗余计数与流水对账。

    冗余字段（item.used_times / item.used_qty / sale.used_quota）
    必须等于 SUM(use_qty)。差异 > 0 立即告警并自动修复冗余值，
    绝不去改只增流水表。
    """
    from app.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m10.member_reconcile_service import MemberReconcileService

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        svc = MemberReconcileService(db)
        diffs = await svc.find_quota_mismatch()
        for row in diffs:
            logger.error(
                "quota_mismatch sale_no=%s redundant=%s ledger=%s diff=%s",
                row["sale_no"], row["redundant_used"], row["ledger_used"], row["diff"],
            )
        fixed = await svc.fix_quota_mismatch(diffs)
        return {"mismatch": len(diffs), "fixed": fixed}
```
